Students described Beacon's responses as closely aligned with module content and more trustworthy than unrestricted generative AI tools, valuing its use of pseudocode and scaffolded explanations over direct solutions. Although participants remained cautious about trusting AI-generated responses without verification, they viewed the system as a valuable first point of support before consulting lecturers or official resources. The findings suggest that carefully designed course-specific AI systems may reduce barriers to academic support by occupying an intermediary space between independent study and formal support. Rather than replacing educators, educational AI may be most valuable when it broadens access to guidance while preserving the pedagogical role of lecturers.

</details>


### [184] [Calibrating Teacher--Student Discrepancy for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21619)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li,MingCai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Cal-OPD，通过正负特权干预估计教师自身偏差区域，并校准teacher-student差异，仅保留超出该区域的部分作为优化信号，在数学推理任务上以约52-65%的信号量持续超越标准OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准on-policy distillation中观察到的teacher-student差异不仅包含能力差距，还混杂了教师自身的偏差；在privileged OPD中，特权信息会放大教师侧似然偏移，使学生学到更多教师偏差。

Method: 提出Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)，通过正向和负向特权干预估计教师的self-deviation区域，在此基础上校准原始teacher-student差异，仅保留超出该区域的分量作为优化信号。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Cal-OPD仅使用原teacher-student差异的约52-65%作为优化信号，却在不同模型规模上持续优于标准OPD及其变体。

Conclusion: 去除教师自身偏差、只保留真实能力差距的校准信号，能更高效且更有效地进行蒸馏，为on-policy distillation提供了一种更可靠的优化目标。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning models by learning the token-level discrepancy between a stronger teacher and an on-policy student. However, this discrepancy does not purely reflect the capability gap between the teacher and the student: it also contains deviations arising from the teacher itself, which are consequently mixed into the observed teacher--student discrepancy and indiscriminately learned by standard OPD during training. This issue is further exacerbated by privileged OPD, where privileged information induces larger teacher-side likelihood shifts, thereby encouraging the student to learn more of the teacher's own deviation. We introduce \textbf{Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)}, which estimates the teacher's self-deviation region through positive and negative privileged interventions and calibrates the original teacher--student discrepancy by retaining only the component that lies beyond this region. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [185] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Self-Meta-Evolve分层框架，为每个用户维护专属prompt并通过双循环持续优化，配合292用户基准，成功率74.58%，超基线13.56点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业IE场景中同一文档需针对不同用户重组，但现有prompt优化方法只用单一prompt对全局目标优化，忽略了真实职场的用户异质性。

Method: 将企业IE建模为交互反馈下的per-user prompt适配；Self-Meta-Evolve为每个用户维护独立prompt，双循环：内循环基于persona条件反馈编辑结构化prompt，外循环通过蒸馏成功编辑模式进化meta-prompt；发布基于O*NET的292个模拟企业用户基准。

Result: 基准上成功率74.58%，超最强prompt优化基线13.56个百分点；仅两轮迭代即达52.54%；20位真实从业者双盲研究显示71%成对比较中优于静态基线。

Conclusion: 面向用户异质性的个性化prompt适配优于全局单一prompt，人机评测均验证其有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [186] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 L0-MoE，一种用 L0 正则化的轻量级 MoE 方法，在几乎不损失性能的情况下加速稠密 LLM，最高实现 2.5 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理慢且昂贵；已有加速方法常带来明显性能下降，而 MoE 模型又需要大量计算资源。

Method: 采用 L0 正则化的轻量级 MoE 方法；引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知的数据集筛选，并使用动态批处理实现高效训练。

Result: 相比稠密模型最高实现 2.5 倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，优于现有 LLM 加速基线。

Conclusion: L0-MoE 能在几乎不损失性能的前提下有效加速稠密 LLM，是一种轻量且高效的加速方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [187] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GUARD 方法，将大推理模型的不安全披露转化为"自然遗忘轨迹"，并引入 NFRS 评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）的思维链（CoT）中间步骤可能暴露受保护事实或不安全推理，现有遗忘方法仅抑制目标内容或改写内部表示，未规定遗忘后轨迹应如何延续，导致幻觉替代、边界错乱或重复输出。

Method: GUARD 将模型生成的不安全披露转化为安全退出轨迹（连贯的非披露 CoT + 稳定的拒绝式回答），通过引导 token 对齐冻结的 LRM，并将该行为蒸馏进模型参数。同时提出 NFRS 指标，从结构稳定性、流畅度和无依据替代内容三方面评估替代质量。

Result: 在 R-TOFU 和基于 STAR-1 构造的有害意图场景上，GUARD 在两种主流蒸馏 LRM 上大幅减少不安全与隐私披露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRM 遗忘应学习"自然遗忘轨迹"，GUARD 通过引导蒸馏实现该目标，NFRS 为超出泄漏之外的替代质量提供评估手段，兼顾安全性与推理能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [188] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReACT-TTS两阶段框架，利用听者一秒预反应面部序列规划对话回复的情绪与韵律，再经Grad-TTS实现语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音依赖对话上下文及听者紧邻的前一行为，需建模听者的即时反应以提升回复规划的合理性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用一秒听者预反应面部序列（时间建模）预测下一话语的情绪与韵律，对比Text-only及各视觉变体并做消融；第二阶段将预测的回复风格接入Grad-TTS骨干实现端到端语音合成。

Result: 严格MELD双人协议下，时间条件在十个随机种子上取得更高mean macro-F1与VAD一致性，准确率基本持平；消融显示时间建模在视觉变体中最佳，显式早晚差无必要，正确听者反应平均优于循环错配。20位语音研究者评估中76%偏好Temporal，9%偏好Text-only，15%无偏好。

Conclusion: 听者预反应动态可作为对话回复规划的互补线索，代码已开源。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [189] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transformer的行为失败不代表缺乏世界模型。TaxiGPT案例中，模型实际学会了忠实表征环境，失败源于交叉路口特征的叠加干扰，而非内部地图混乱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正以行为失败推断模型缺乏世界模型的误解，主张从"是否有世界模型"转向机制性研究其世界建模过程。

Method: 对在曼哈顿随机游走上训练的TaxiGPT进行机制分析与因果干预；提出affordance packing方法；设计机制性指标比较模型。

Result: 模型能表征路口与街道、追踪位置、利用目标罗盘导航；失败源于叠加的交叉路口特征相互干扰导致定位错误；affordance packing可限制错误后果；世界建模能力在训练不同阶段涌现。

Conclusion: 应从"模型是否有世界模型"转向机制性地研究世界建模——即表征环境并用以指导行为的相互作用能力。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [190] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文识别出多模态临床预测中的"ECG Mirage"现象（表观多模态能力但未真正利用患者ECG信息），并提出视觉提示微调作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊决策依赖病史、生命体征、化验和心电图等异质信息，VLM虽能联合处理，但强预测性能未必意味着真正利用了正确患者的ECG信息。

Method: 在MDS-ED上对比匹配ECG、结果不一致错配ECG和无图像输入三种条件下的预测，保持文本与预测目标不变；随后在冻结VLM主干的前提下，用监督学习加条件直接偏好优化训练四个受限视觉提示。

Result: 四个VLM中匹配ECG对ICU入住或病情恶化预测均无一致优势；新方法达到ICU入住70.6%、恶化预测67.5%的平衡准确率，并将匹配对错配的性能差距分别提升至约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多模态临床预测中的ECG Mirage问题，并证明视觉提示微调是一种高效的缓解手段。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [191] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 简单统计流程在单变量时序异常检测上可达0.529 VUS-PR，超越公开榜单最佳神经与统计条目，接近预训练基础模型；候选特征池决定性能上限，用LLM生成候选池并与其并集选择可将性能提升至0.588。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格选择协议下，简单统计流程在单变量时序异常检测中能达到何种水平，并识别性能瓶颈所在。

Method: 在滑动窗口上提取小规模统计量池，用转导鲁棒（MAD）模型打分，并在留出调参集上按域选择特征子集；进一步通过多模态LLM结合域内示例窗口生成候选池，并与手工池取并集选择。

Result: TSB-AD-U上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，高于最佳神经（0.45）与统计（0.44）条目，距最强预训练基础模型0.06。消融显示：选择策略/事后oracle影响0.031，聚合网格0.096，而候选池变化影响0.226。LLM生成池在匹配选择下与手工池相当且覆盖不同域，并集选择在全部12组生成器-种子对上均提升，达到0.588。

Conclusion: 候选池设定性能上限，池上搜索是二阶因素；结合LLM生成池与手工池可匹配榜单最佳条目，说明简单统计流程经合适特征池设计具有强竞争力。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [192] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIST，一种通过基因组引导的组织学注意力机制进行多模态生存预测的模型，在多个外部队列上优于标准融合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型可融合全切片图像与基因组信息，但面临外部队列偏移和计算复杂度高的挑战，有效融合仍然困难。

Method: 将基因组特征表示为 token，使其在生存预测前查询由基础模型导出的紧凑组织学上下文 token，从而在分子信息中融入组织学上下文，而非仅在最终阶段拼接两种模态。训练结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout 以及配对的 WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾脏、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部队列评估中，MIST 在主要对比中相较标准融合基线提升了外部 C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑有效的多模态肿瘤预后预测策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [193] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EnterpriseVal——面向企业用例的评估系统，通过形式化用例规范、多维指标体系、校准评分与双层阈值门将模型能力转化为部署决策，并在银行三流程试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型已能产出专家级交付物，但大多数企业GenAI项目无业务成效、agentic项目多被取消。核心原因是评测错位：公开benchmark回答"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需回答"该工作流在本地数据与管控下是否适配、可靠、安全、值得扩展"。

Method: EnterpriseVal系统含五部分：(i)用例及冻结社会技术配置(模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏、人工监督)的形式化规范，以自主性等级与后果层级决定评估强度；(ii)覆盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保证与监督的指标目录；(iii)通过预测驱动推断(PPI)用校准LLM-as-judge扩展盲评专家判断的评分协议；(iv)以可执行算法表述的双层阈值门，将带置信界的指标向量映射为REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE；(v)以审核者捕获率为可测参数的价值-风险模型。

Result: 某全球银行三工作流试点：信贷备忘录起草中最佳模型人工评分引用精确率88%、幻觉率1.6%，优于门槛70%与5%；流程转换中分析师精修工时从估计27.4小时/文档降至2.9小时。

Conclusion: 论文区分已确立结果、试点证据、提出的系统与开放假设，并给出全面验证所需实验。EnterpriseVal将企业AI评估从能力基准转向可决策的用例级评估。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [194] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEL，一个面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，用问答-检索全环路的排序反馈优化提问策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互检索是序贯信息获取问题，但现有系统通过模仿离线候选QA对排序来训练提问决策，而问题价值实际由回答及对检索的下游影响决定，监督信号弱。

Method: 指出现有候选区分性和感知有用性仅提供弱监督；提出RAVEL，从监督式问题生成初始化，直接观察当前Top-4候选，并用完整问答-检索环路的排序反馈以在线强化学习优化提问策略。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES上，RAVEL在五轮交互中逐步提升检索性能；分析显示其将提问预算重新分配到局部化开放式属性，提供更有用的检索证据，对初始困难查询增益最大。

Conclusion: 用检索排序反馈直接优化提问策略优于离线模仿，能有效提升交互式行人重识别表现。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [195] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem通过写时构建自配置的互补语义视图来组织长期记忆，避免检索时的语义干扰，在保持简单推理流程下提升长程问答与个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统依赖固定粒度或静态模式，面对偏好、事件、约束、时序更新等异构信息混在单一表示中时产生语义干扰，导致top-K检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排名靠后。

Method: 提出数据驱动的AutoViewMem框架：从交互轨迹中发现候选视图，选出紧凑互补视图集，用其指导写入时的结构化提取生成有来源依据的记忆；将语义解耦从检索时提前到写入时，并辅以离线整合提升记忆紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在LoCoMo和PersonaMem基准、Qwen3-8B与Qwen3-14B骨干上，AutoViewMem在长程问答和个性化任务上优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单推理管线。

Conclusion: 表示优先的设计能有效缓解混合记忆的语义干扰，无需显式路由或迭代检索即可用标准top-K检索获取聚焦证据，为LLM长期记忆提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [196] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在运动应激与跨会话条件下系统评估ECG生物特征识别，采用Siamese ResNet与多导联晚期融合，在真实场景中取得低EER并验证了心脏签名的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG蕴含个体特异模式，可用于身份识别，并有助于保护心脏敏感数据及作为自监督学习的预训练任务。但现有研究多局限于单会话静息数据，对时间与生理变化下的鲁棒性缺乏验证。

Method: 构建基于心肺运动试验的大型ECG数据集，训练带晚期多导联融合策略的Siamese ResNet，并采用运动与时间感知评估协议，同时在公开基准（含CYBHi）上测试。

Result: 在生理与时间双重变化下，会话内静息到峰值状态的EER为1.7%；在CYBHi数据集上达到3.9%的当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 结果支持存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏签名，表明ECG生物特征识别在真实动态条件下具备可行性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [197] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PIR方法，通过读取模型内部状态识别其"认出"的正确答案，无需参考模型或标注数据，能区分模型"不愿回答"与"不能回答"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能隐藏其真实掌握的知识（如评估中故意怠工或给出与内部认知相悖的答案），仅凭输出无法判断模型是在隐藏答案还是根本不知道答案。

Method: 借鉴取证学中的隐藏信息测试（CIT）：向模型呈现问题及候选答案，从模型内部状态中读出它认为哪个候选答案是正确的。PIR无需参考模型、无需标注真值语料。

Result: 在5个系列（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）共8个模型上，PIR以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复被识别的答案，远高于未知项基线0.28–0.40和随机率0.25；在提示欺骗、训练怠工、密码锁定及电路破坏等各类隐藏形式下识别率保持0.85–0.93。

Conclusion: PIR能区分"不愿回答"与"不能回答"的模型，支持怠工审计与遗忘验证；该信号具有因果性，提供超越黑盒行为线索的信息，并可扩展至自由生成任务。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [198] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CodeMidas：一种以源代码为唯一输入的智能体流水线，将现有代码库中已实现的功能转化为可执行的RL环境，构建了5,545个训练任务（涵盖3,185个代码库、23种语言、15个领域），训练后显著提升编码智能体在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过RL训练编码智能体需要多样任务与可靠验证器。现有方法依赖issues和commits等开发痕迹，限制了任务提取范围和RL环境的规模化。

Method: CodeMidas，一种智能体流水线，仅以源代码为任务特定输入，在各阶段分配智能体计算：探索已实现功能以制定行为规格、基于原始代码执行构建测试、通过执行检查与重复解回滚验证过滤候选任务。

Result: 产出5,545个训练任务，来自3,185个开源代码库，覆盖23种编程语言与15个技术领域。用GRPO训练MiMo-V2.5后，在五个基准上全部提升：DeepSWE +11.7%、ProgramBench +17%、Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%。消融显示高质量任务数量增加可提升性能；轨迹分析表明RL训练智能体代码库探索增加、自验证更多样。

Conclusion: 源代码可作为构建RL环境的可扩展基础，能跨多样软件任务提升编码智能体能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [199] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"程序性记忆"框架，让冻结的前沿模型通过230+工具操作专业设计软件，从经验中积累并精炼自然语言设计技能，实现持续自适应，无需权重更新或人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长周期智能体任务，结构化可编辑产物由大量相互依赖动作产生，但结果缺乏可靠的程序化评估标准（无法用自动 oracle 判断好坏）。

Method: 外部自然语言技能的程序性记忆：通过"加宽"（为反复出现的未覆盖子任务获取新程序）和"加深"（基于自身成功/失败执行修订已有程序）来演进；并用匹配回放门控，仅接受能修复失败且不损害已有成功的改动。模型冻结、无权重更新、无人工标注。

Result: 在1,406个真实用户brief和1,869条自动评分轨迹上迭代5轮，技能库从76增至139。GenEval2执行成功率在Claude-Sonnet-4上从72.7%升至99.3%（生成质量+11.99分）；在四个专业设计基准上对无技能智能体的胜率达61.8%（Sonnet-4）和67.6%（Opus-4.6）。在200个留出brief上，单独加宽/加深胜率为49.4%/48.6%，二者结合达58.5%（p=0.025）。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为在噪声大、不可验证反馈下的智能体持续自适应提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Helpful but Fallible: Developer Experiences of AI Tools Under a Coordinated Industrial Roll-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20977)
*Andreas Bexell,Rushali Gupta,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma Söderberg,Per Runeson,Sigrid Eldh,Wei Shi,Konstantin Malysh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项针对瑞典大型电信公司协调部署AI开发工具的案例研究，通过12名开发者的访谈揭示：部署体验与预期存在管理层与开发者之间的"期望落差"，且感知风险是现有接受模型（TAM2）未涵盖的重要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发工具正被工业界广泛采用，人们对生产力提升抱有强烈期望，但开发者自身的部署体验研究不足。现有证据多来自工具评估、生产力指标和问卷调查，缺乏对持续、协调部署的定性现场描述，导致组织在预算、员工评估和实践调整上基于片面的图景决策。

Method: 对瑞典一家大型电信公司的AI开发工具协调部署开展案例研究，覆盖三个站点、访谈12名软件专业人员，采用半结构化访谈，使用过程编码与主题分析，并以扩展技术接受模型（TAM2）作为事后分析视角。

Result: 访谈发现使用场景、生产力、挫折感与工具局限等方面的结果与既有调查相互印证；此外还揭示了管理层与开发者之间的期望落差，对应TAM2中的主观规范与自愿性构念，并显示参与者高度重视感知风险——这是TAM2及类似接受模型未能体现的因素。

Conclusion: AI开发工具是有帮助但易出错的助手，其价值受组织期望、系统规模和开发者技能共同塑造。研究提示现有接受模型需纳入感知风险等维度，组织部署时应关注管理-开发者期望对齐。

Abstract: AI-enabled software development tools (AI-devtools) are being industrially adopted under strong expectations of productivity gains, yet developers' experiences of such roll-outs are underexplored. Organizations commit budgets, evaluate staff, and revise practice on a partial picture, since the evidence base is mainly tool evaluations, productivity metrics, and surveys, with few qualitative in-situ accounts of ongoing, coordinated roll-outs. We report a case study of a coordinated roll-out of AI-devtools at a large Swedish telecommunications company, investigating how developers experience the roll-out and how they anticipate their profession will change. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 12 software professionals across three sites, analyzed with process coding and thematic analysis, and interpreted through the extended Technology Acceptance Model (TAM2) as a post-hoc analytical lens. Our findings on use cases, productivity, frustrations, and tool limitations corroborate prior survey work. Beyond corroboration, the interviews surface a management-developer expectation gap that maps onto the TAM2 constructs of subjective norm and voluntariness, and show that participants weigh perceived risk heavily, a factor that TAM2 and similar acceptance models do not represent. AI-devtools emerge as helpful but fallible assistants whose value is shaped by organizational expectations, system scale, and developers' skills.

</details>


### [201] [Embedding Drift in Code Vulnerability Models Under Intended Behaviour-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21203)
*Hasti Ghaneshirazi,Tahsin Reza,Ladan Tahvildari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 语义保持的代码变换会使冻结代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，导致漏洞预测翻转。作者在Big-Vul的7500对C/C++函数上测试四种变异，提出train-only防御缩小干净与变异表示距离，将VFR从22.60%降至11.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注释删除、不可达代码、重命名、循环重写等语义中性改动不应改变模型的安全判断，但冻结嵌入下正确检出的漏洞会被误判为良性，这种不稳定性值得研究。

Method: 使用Big-Vul中15000个C/C++函数（7500对漏洞-补丁对），对漏洞与良性样本施加四种变异轨迹及组合设置；采用冻结的microsoft/codebert-base嵌入与六个下游分类器；提出仅训练期防御，将干净与变异表示投影得更近，同时保留漏洞类别信息。

Result: 组合变异下基线VFR为35.55%–42.15%。防御将共同集平均VFR从22.60%降至11.32%，变异准确率从55.33%升至57.10%。Logistic Regression的VFR下降17.29个百分点，但良性→漏洞翻转增加11.95个百分点。

Conclusion: 该防御提升了语义保持代码变换下的鲁棒性，但引入假阳性权衡，且未完全消除预测不稳定性。

Abstract: Code edits that preserve program behaviour can shift frozen code embeddings across classifier decision boundaries, causing correctly detected vulnerabilities to be predicted as benign. Such instability is problematic because semantically neutral changes, including comment removal, insertion of unreachable code, variable renaming, and loop rewriting, should not alter a model's security judgment. We investigate this issue using 15,000 C/C++ functions organized as 7,500 vulnerable-patched pairs from the Big-Vul dataset. Four mutation tracks are applied to both vulnerable and benign samples: comment removal, unreachable code insertion, variable renaming, loop rewriting, and a combined setting that applies all transformations. Using frozen microsoft/codebert-base embeddings with six downstream classifiers, we evaluate the robustness of vulnerability predictions and introduce a train-only defence that projects clean and mutated representations closer together while preserving vulnerability-class information. Under combined mutations, the baseline Vulnerable Flip Rate (VFR) ranges from 35.55% to 42.15%. The proposed defence reduces the mean common-set VFR from 22.60% to 11.32% and improves mean mutated accuracy from 55.33% to 57.10%. For Logistic Regression, VFR decreases by 17.29 percentage points, although benign-to-vulnerable flips increase by 11.95 percentage points. These results indicate that the defence improves robustness to semantics-preserving code transformations but introduces a false-positive trade-off and does not fully eliminate prediction instability.

</details>


### [202] [License Compliance in Open Source Cybersecurity Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21218)
*Ahmed Shah,Selman Selman,Ibrahim Abualhaol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步分析，识别常用许可证类型和语言，发现宽松许可证项目存在被限制性许可证污染的情况，且大量代码缺乏版权归属。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全软件开发依赖开源软件，但在吸收前需检查许可证是否宽松。存在开源包被限制性许可证代码静默污染的风险，可能导致商业产品面临高昂补救成本、声誉损失和法律费用。

Method: 对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步分析，识别最常用的许可证类型和编程语言，并寻找宽松许可证项目被限制性许可证材料污染的迹象。

Result: 分析发现宽松许可证开源项目中存在限制性许可证污染案例，同时发现高比例的代码缺乏版权归属。

Conclusion: 该研究预期帮助管理者和开发者理解污染如何发生；帮助开源社区通过添加许可证和版权信息更好地保护知识产权；帮助创业者理解开源网络安全领域的许可证与污染问题及其对软件架构决策的影响。

Abstract: Developers of cybersecurity software often include and rely upon open source software packages in their commercial software products. Before open source code is absorbed into a proprietary product, developers must check the package license to see if the project is permissively licensed, thereby allowing for commercial-friendly inheritance and redistribution. However, there is a risk that the open source package license could be inaccurate due to being silently contaminated with restrictively licensed open source code that may prohibit the sale or confidentiality of commercial derivative work. Contamination of commercial products could lead to expensive remediation costs, damage to the company's reputation, and costly legal fees. In this article, we report on our preliminary analysis of more than 200 open source cybersecurity projects to identify the most frequently used license types and languages and to look for evidence of permissively licensed open source projects that are likely contaminated by restrictive licensed material (i.e., containing commercial-unfriendly code). Our analysis identified restrictive license contamination cases occurring in permissively licensed open source projects. Furthermore, we found a high proportion of code that lacked copyright attribution. We expect that the results of this study will: i) provide managers and developers with an understanding of how contamination can occur, ii) provide open source communities with an understanding on how they can better protect their intellectual property by including licenses and copyright information in their code, and iii) provide entrepreneurs with an understanding of the open source cybersecurity domain in terms of licensing and contamination and how they affect decisions about cybersecurity software architectures.

</details>


### [203] [What Stops a Small Language Model From Driving a Database Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21341)
*Cevheri Bozoglan,Yusuf Gundogdu,Abdullah Kaya,Koray Sirin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过在开源SQL客户端agent模式上运行39个开源权重模型（8199次运行），论文发现所谓小模型无法胜任agentic数据库工作的假设不成立：75.7%的agent模式失败来自已调用工具的运行，而非能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设小型开源权重模型缺乏推理能力，因而无法完成agentic数据库任务。作者用一个生产系统来检验这一假设，探究失败究竟源于模型能力还是系统本身。

Method: 11天内驱动开源SQL客户端的agent模式，本地部署39个开源权重模型加1个托管对照，覆盖6类任务面，共8199次运行、110711条台账事件、14008次被拒工具调用。对2100次归因于模型的损失进行聚类重采样分析，并捕获此前不可见的工具参数以定位传输失败。

Result: 2100次模型归因损失中1590次（75.7%）来自已调用工具的运行，该多数在99.7%的聚类重采样中成立。其中传输失败（用了工具但未交付）最大占36.2%，能力失败（未调用任何工具）最小占17.3%。传输失败可分解为几种机械性参数形态，并暴露出5个服务器缺陷。仅5处服务器改动即让6个模型在30格中移动6到21格。另报告一个混淆因素：无上下文上限时，一个7.1GB模型占满262144 token窗口、在64GB机器上占用51GB，其运行与模型超时在普通日志中无法区分。

Conclusion: agentic数据库工作中大部分失败源于系统/传输机制而非模型能力，小型开源模型并非先天不能胜任。作者的混淆发现也暗示已发表的本地模型基准可能受到影响。语料、评分器及可复现所有图表的验证器已公开。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models are assumed to fail at agentic database work because they lack the reasoning capacity for it. We test that against a production system. Over eleven days we drove the agent mode of an open-source SQL client with 39 open-weight models served locally and one hosted control, across six task surfaces: 8,199 runs, 110,711 ledger events, 14,008 refused tool calls. Of the 2,100 model-attributed agent-mode losses, 1,590, or 75.7%, came from runs that had invoked at least one tool. That majority is what survives resampling models rather than runs: it holds in 99.7% of clustered resamples and in 15 of the 22 models with at least twenty losses. Within it, transport, a run that used the tools and never got a deliverable through, is the largest class at 36.2% and capability, a run that invoked no tool at all, the smallest at 17.3%; we report that ordering as a property of this corpus rather than a general finding, since clustered by model it holds in only 74.5% of resamples. Transport failures decompose into a few mechanical argument shapes. Production ledgers record refusal codes and never the model's arguments, so these were invisible for ten days; capturing them exposed five server defects, one of which demanded a field on one tool, forbade it on the sibling that composed it, then failed the run for its absence. Five server changes, touching no model, prompt or sampling setting, moved six models by 6 to 21 cells out of 30. We also report a confound we believe affects published local-model benchmarks, ours included: with no context cap, one 7.1 GB model was admitted at its full 262,144-token window and held 51 GB on a 64 GB machine, producing runs indistinguishable in any ordinary log from a model timing out. The corpus, the scorer and a verifier that regenerates every figure are released.

</details>


### [204] [Cross-Platform vs Native Mobile Development: An Empirical Study of Software Quality Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21544)
*Alexandru Ilovan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较同一植物管理应用的五种实现（原生iOS、原生Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform），共八个可执行变体，基于ISO/IEC 25010评估时间行为、实现足迹、源码组织与渲染响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台移动框架虽承诺代码复用、缩短交付周期、降低实现成本，但与原生开发的权衡难以客观评估。现有比较常依赖简化应用、不一致的功能集或狭窄的度量指标。

Method: 五种实现共享同一领域、后端服务、功能需求与基准契约，产出八个可执行变体。每变体提供2000次完整运行数据，覆盖认证与缓存检索、图像传输与解码、列表渲染与滚动、本地同步及媒体上传；后端准备与框架特定UI驱动程序单独分析。

Result: 原生在两个操作系统上记录最低的非UI客户端小计，Kotlin Multiplatform是最接近的跨平台实现；操作与UI包装排名因任务而异。共享方案编写的移动端源码少于原生应用总和，源码清单显示不同的文件大小、组织与依赖使用模式。

Conclusion: 研究未发现普遍最优技术；各方案的优势与成本取决于质量属性、工作负载、平台及度量边界。

Abstract: Cross-platform mobile frameworks promise code reuse, shorter delivery cycles, and lower implementation effort, but their trade-offs relative to native development remain difficult to assess objectively. Many comparisons rely on simplified applications, inconsistent feature sets, or a narrow set of metrics. This paper compares five implementations of the same plant-management application: native iOS, native Android, Flutter, React Native, and Kotlin Multiplatform. The shared approaches target both Android and iOS, yielding eight executable variants. Guided by ISO/IEC 25010, the study examines time behavior, implementation footprint, source-code organization, and observable rendering responsiveness.
  All implementations share the same domain, backend services, functional requirements, and benchmark contract. The supplied dataset contains 2,000 completed runs per variant and covers authenticated and cached retrieval, image transfer and decoding, list rendering and scrolling, local synchronization, and media upload. Backend preparation and framework-specific UI drivers are analyzed separately from the primary client workflow.
  Native records the lowest non-UI client subtotal on both operating systems, with Kotlin Multiplatform the closest cross-platform implementation, while operation and UI-wrapper rankings vary by task. The shared approaches contain less authored mobile source than the native applications combined, and the source inventory shows different patterns of file size, organization, and dependency use. Rather than identifying a universally superior technology, the study shows that each approach's advantages and costs depend on the quality attribute, workload, platform, and measurement boundary.

</details>


### [205] [GameLogicBench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Runtime Game Logic with Tick-Level State Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21562)
*Xinyu Che,Yunfei Ge,Shihao Li,Yanchen Liu,Hang Yan,Xinping Lei,Yanghai Wang,Zixuan Dong,Yifan Yao,Qianqian Xie,Letian Zhu,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出GameLogicBench，一个包含72个Godot游戏逻辑任务的基准，通过在每帧检查游戏规则、跨403个场景与1451个种子变体测试用例来评估编码智能体，最佳组合仅解决52.78%任务，并强调变异体验证与网络访问控制对可靠评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏开发基准只重放固定示例、评分视频或让模型评判，无法在多样场景中全程检查游戏规则，也无法保证判定可完全复现。需要一个能衡量真实游戏逻辑实现质量、且对不同正确实现保持鲁棒的基准。

Method: 构建GameLogicBench：72个Godot玩法逻辑任务，覆盖孤立机制、多系统交互和仓库级功能；自动评估器在每个模拟tick检查规则；403个人工设计场景经种子参数生成1451个测试用例；用变异体（移除某项必需能力的实现）验证评估器，确保其接受不同正确实现但拒绝缺陷实现。

Result: 在20种语言模型与脚手架组合中，最佳解决率52.78%；Claude Code下所有12个模型随任务范围扩大（孤立机制→交互系统→仓库级）解决率下降；仓库级任务中智能体更频繁查看代码、调用更多工具；多数失败提交可运行但部分行为实现错误；未使用变异体验证时错误提交会通过；开放网络时智能体会复制公开仓库代码。

Conclusion: 可靠的编码智能体评估同时取决于测试拒绝什么（变异体验证）以及智能体能访问哪些外部代码（网络访问控制）。GameLogicBench为游戏逻辑这一复杂领域提供了可复现、抗实现差异的评估方法。

Abstract: Coding agents can modify and test code across large software projects. Game development is a domain where agents must implement gameplay rules. A game can end in a valid state even after violating its rules during the run. Current game-development benchmarks replay fixed examples, score videos, or ask another model to judge the result. However, no existing benchmark checks game rules throughout execution across varied evaluator-selected scenarios while ensuring exactly reproducible verdicts. We introduce GameLogicBench, a benchmark of 72 gameplay-logic tasks in Godot projects. An automated evaluator checks each game's rules at every simulation tick. Across 403 hand-designed scenarios, seeded parameter variations produce 1,451 test cases. To ensure that the evaluator measures behavior rather than implementation choice, it must accept different correct implementations for each task while rejecting mutants, implementations with one required capability removed. The tasks span isolated mechanics, multi-system interactions, and repository-scale features. Across 20 combinations of language models and scaffolds, the best observed run solves 52.78% of tasks. Under Claude Code, all twelve models solve fewer tasks as task scope expands from isolated mechanics, through interacting systems, to repository-scale features. Agents inspect code more often and make more tool calls on repository-scale tasks than on isolated-mechanic tasks. Most unsuccessful submissions are runnable, but implement some required game behavior incorrectly. We compared versions of our benchmark evaluator built with and without validation using mutants. Without this validation, incorrect agent submissions passed. A separate analysis finds agents copying code from public repositories when network access is open. Reliable evaluation thus depends both on what the tests reject and on what external code agents can access.

</details>


### [206] [Labelling Bug-Fixing Commits with Local Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21616)
*Philip König,Georg Goldenits,Caroline König,Sebastian Raubitzek,Fabian Obermann,Dennis Toth,David Schmidt,Edgar Weippl,Kevin Mallinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究能否仅凭提交内容（message+diff）用本地开源权重语言模型将提交标记为bug修复，并用多语言人工验证语料评估。结果显示关键词基线召回不足一半，开源模型能恢复绝大多数修复并显著优于基线，且模型越大不一定越好；同时指出无负例语料无法支持精度比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷预测依赖识别修复bug的提交，但现有标签来源各有噪声：复用基准有数据质量问题，issue链接有偏且报告常被误分类，关键词匹配过于粗糙。因此需要一种不依赖issue tracker、可本地运行、可复现且适用于专有代码的提交级标注方法。

Method: 在涵盖Java、Python、JavaScript的人工验证与精选bug修复数据集上，比较关键词基线与不同规模的开源权重模型；每个模型同时输入提交消息和代码diff进行提示，以判断该提交是否为bug修复。

Result: 在人工验证语料上，关键词基线只能找回不到一半的修复，而开源权重模型能找回绝大多数，并在每个仓库上均以统计显著性优于基线；更大模型并未持续优于更小模型。研究还表明，不含负例的评估语料无法支持精度感知的分类器比较。

Conclusion: 提交内容本身足以用本地开源模型高召回调标注bug修复，是可复现、低成本、可用于专有代码且独立于issue tracker的方案；作者发布标注流水线及按推荐配置生成的多语言银标准标注语料，便于构建当前项目特定数据集。

Abstract: Defect prediction depends on knowing which commits fix bugs, yet the labels that encode this are produced by routes that each introduce noise. Reused benchmarks carry documented data-quality problems, issue-tracker links are biased and the underlying reports are frequently mistyped, and matching keywords in commit messages is a coarse heuristic. This paper examines whether commits can be labelled as bug fixes from their content alone, using open-weight language models that run locally and therefore keep the process reproducible, inexpensive at corpus scale, usable on proprietary code, and independent of any issue tracker. Against datasets of manually validated and curated bug fixes spanning Java, Python, and JavaScript, we compare a keyword baseline with a set of open-weight models of varying size, prompting each with the commit message and the code diff. On the manually validated corpus the keyword baseline recovers fewer than half of the fixes, whereas the open-weight models recover the large majority and outperform it repository by repository with statistical significance, and larger models do not consistently outperform smaller ones. We further show that evaluation corpora without negative examples cannot support a precision-aware comparison of such classifiers. We release the labelling pipeline together with a labelled, multi-language corpus produced by the recommended configuration, as a reproducible silver-standard resource for building current, project-specific datasets.

</details>


### [207] [From Smarter to Hungrier: the Role of Energy Efficiency in Software-defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21643)
*Ella Peltonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 软件定义汽车带来智能化的同时也加剧能耗，本文探讨其对可持续性和环境伦理的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代汽车从机械实体转向软件定义设备，传感器、算力和网络能力提升，支撑更复杂的算法，但也需要更多能源运行机器学习和AI功能，由此引发可持续性和环境伦理问题。

Method: 本文为讨论/立场类论文，分析引入高资源消耗软硬件组件与社会、可持续性之间的平衡，并呼吁新型工具来测量、管理和优化能耗及其他可持续性指标。

Result: 论文指出软件定义汽车的发展对可持续性构成威胁，尤其体现在能源消耗的增加上，需要在功能与环保之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 需要新的工具与设计指南来衡量、管理和优化能耗及可持续性指标，并提供可操作的建议，以应对软件定义汽车的可持续发展挑战。

Abstract: Automated features and advanced driving support systems have enabled a range of life-saving and comfort applications in modern personal vehicles. Development from mostly mechanical entities towards truly software-defined devices also means that added sensor complexity, processing power, and networking capabilities provide a platform for running even more complex algorithms and applications. As vehicles become smarter, they require even more energy to enable advanced machine learning- and artificial intelligence-based functionalities. This paper discusses the implications for sustainability and the threats to environmental ethics posed by the development of software-defined vehicles. We discuss the balance between introducing more resource-hungry software and hardware components and their effect on society and sustainability. We underscore the need for novel tools to measure, manage, and optimise energy consumption and other sustainability indicators, with design guidance and actionable recommendations.

</details>


### [208] [Supporting Industrial Test-Failure Analysis with LLM-Based Systems: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21843)
*Eric Jansson,Per Strandberg,Thomas Sörensen,Eduard Paul Enoiu,Wasif Afzal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究工具增强LLM在Westermo夜间测试失败根因分析(RCA)中的应用，对比单智能体与多智能体编排配置。工业案例研究表明单智能体更快更省，多智能体无明显质量优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间测试产生异构数据与日志，需人工跨多源排查，效率低。论文旨在探索LLM+工具自动化RCA的可行性及智能体架构的适用性。

Method: 实现单智能体与多智能体编排的RCA工作流，均可访问测试元数据和日志；基于两个真实失败场景开展探索性工业案例研究，6名从业者通过问卷和焦点小组评估，另采集120次重复执行的运行指标。

Result: 两种配置在两个场景中均未表现出一致的从业者感知质量优势；单智能体生成报告更快、成本更低，是更实用的基线。评估涵盖正确性、推理质量、修复现实性、清晰度、有用性、信任度及成本、时长、一致性。

Conclusion: 在此背景下单智能体是更实际的方案；多智能体架构的潜在优势需在更复杂场景中进一步评估。

Abstract: This study examines tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) systems for supporting Root Cause Analysis (RCA) of nightly test failures at Westermo Network Technologies AB. Nightly test executions produce heterogeneous test data and logs that practitioners currently inspect manually across multiple sources. We implemented an RCA workflow in single-agent and orchestrated multi-agent configurations, both with access to test metadata and logs. An exploratory industrial case study used two real failure scenarios. Six practitioners evaluated the scenario reports through a survey and focus group, and operational measurements were collected from 120 repeated executions. The evaluation covered practitioner-perceived correctness, reasoning quality, fix realism, clarity, usefulness, and trust, as well as cost, duration, and consistency. Neither configuration showed a consistent practitioner-perceived quality advantage across the two scenarios. The single agent system generated reports faster and at lower cost, making it the more practical baseline in this context. The potential benefits of agent architectures require further evaluation in more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [209] [How Researchers Use and Verify AI Coding Assistants: Tasks and Validation Practices in Scientific Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22049)
*Gabrielle O'Brien,Reed Milewicz,Nasir Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项2025年针对科研编程者的调查（527份自由文本）显示，研究者主要将生成式AI用于五类任务，且代码评估方式极为非正式和个人化，评估信心主要取决于对AI和自身的信心，而非所用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已进入科研编程，但缺乏证据说明研究者把哪些任务交给它、以及他们如何判断生成代码是否正确。

Method: 分析2025年调查中527份自由文本回应，受访者多为美国大学写代码的研究者。每位受访者描述一项任务、AI使用方式及评估结果的做法。对任务与评估策略进行编码，并与编程经验、研究领域和信心评分关联。

Result: 使用集中于五类任务：数据处理、可视化、调试、数学/科学计算、统计分析。评估非正式且个人化：超半数提到运行生成代码，自动化测试和他人审查罕见。用例与评估策略随编程经验变化不大，但信心有差异——经验少的更信任AI而非自己，经验多的相反。评估信心与所用策略无关，最强相关因素是对工具和自身的信心。

Conclusion: 对AI在科学代码中贡献的验证很大程度上依赖个人判断，缺乏共享的测试或审查基础设施。界面可支持与任务相匹配的评估，而非将其完全留给用户。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered research programming, yet there is little evidence about which tasks researchers hand to it or how they decide whether its code is correct. We draw on 527 free-text responses to a 2025 survey of researchers who write code, most of them at U.S. universities. In each response, a researcher recounts a single task from their own work, the way they used an AI tool for it, and what they did to assess the result. We coded the task and the evaluation strategies reported, and related both to programming experience, research area, and confidence ratings. Use was concentrated in five tasks: data handling, visualization, debugging, mathematical/scientific computing, and statistical analysis. Evaluation was informal and individual. Over half of accounts described running the generated code, while automated tests and review by another person were rare. Use cases and evaluation strategies varied little with programming experience, but confidence did: Less experienced programmers trusted the AI more than themselves, and experienced programmers the reverse. Evaluation confidence was not associated with the strategies reported. Its strongest correlates were confidence in the tool and in oneself. Validating AI contributions to scientific code rested largely on individual judgment, outside shared infrastructure for testing or review. Interfaces could support task-appropriate evaluation rather than leave it to the user.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"稀疏先验"与"稀疏维度"概念，证明在此先验下分布学习风险可达Ω(√(k/n))并给出匹配上界，从而克服对n的维度诅咒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI学习d维分布的样本复杂度为O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，虽最优但过于悲观；仅靠平滑性假设不足以刻画真实分布结构。

Method: 引入稀疏先验类，定义"稀疏维度"(Sparse Dimension)度量先验稀疏性；证明分布学习与Bayesian场景下"学习采样"的统计等价性。

Result: 在k-稀疏先验下，贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))；在温和附加假设下，TV距离上界与之匹配（忽略对数项）。

Conclusion: 合适的先验可克服关于样本量n的维度诅咒；结果也适用于学习采样任务。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [211] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端BI基准BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%，通过工具增强的BI-Agent结合SFT与RL后训练，最高提升40个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流需用户手动完成识别表、数据转换、构建连接等繁琐准备步骤才能回答业务问题，过程复杂耗时；而LLM在数据处理上能力强大，值得研究其能否端到端完成BI任务。

Method: 从公开来源采集真实BI项目并人工抽取(问题, 真实答案)对，构建BI-Bench基准；设计工具增强的BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为搜索、连接、转换等子任务并编排专用数据管理方法；再构建后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，进行SFT和RL训练。

Result: 前沿LLM在BI-Bench上表现差，准确率低于50%；BI-Agent在原始LLM上最高提升40个百分点，后训练后进一步提升最高30个百分点。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中，工具增强推理与领域特定后训练的结合至关重要，为未来研究指明有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [212] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA是一种端到端可训练稀疏注意力架构，通过查询表示预测动态阈值，配合乘性抑制训练策略，在约85%训练稀疏度下达到接近稠密模型质量，并实现高达2.5倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中大规模KV缓存造成严重内存带宽瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力用刚性启发式选择性加载，会丢弃必要上下文导致质量下降。

Method: 从query表示预测动态上下文阈值；训练时对低于阈值的logit进行乘性抑制而非删除以形成平滑均匀注意力底部；推理时硬剪枝无信息KV块、吸收粗粒度GPU块选择带来的附带token；用Triton实现基于缓存几何概率界、O(1)时间筛选KV块的自定义解码核；并提出离线校准算法冻结每头常数阈值消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B预训练模型在约85%训练稀疏度和约38%活跃解码密度下，在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文检索上与稠密注意力相当；消除初始token注意力汇；解码较FlashAttention-2最高2.5倍加速（序列达512K）；校准额外减少27%注意力计算。

Conclusion: ETA在保持稠密模型质量的同时实现硬件加速稀疏解码，兼顾质量与效率，并提供面向领域部署的校准方案。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [213] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bio-MF：一种无潜空间、单步MeanFlow框架，将EEG信号条件生成fNIRS，用于混合MI-BCI，实现快速高保真跨模态合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合MI-BCI（EEG+fNIRS）优于纯EEG系统，但配对采集不便；现有EEG-to-fNIRS跨模态生成方法生成慢、需预训练，且单步生成会降低保真度并引入非生理伪影，难以满足实时场景。

Method: Bio-MF为无潜空间单步MeanFlow框架：直接进行信号空间x预测，将输出转化为MeanFlow速度监督，单次网络评估完成推理；并集成时空交互4D编码、跨模态无分类器引导、噪声级门控FFT正则化。

Result: Dataset 1上EEG+合成fNIRS使ACC相对纯EEG提升HbR 3.37、HbO 4.15个百分点；Dataset 2在未见64通道EEG蒙太奇下提升2.98与2.50个百分点；RTX PRO 6000上单试次生成仅7.0 ms，较1000步SCDM提速857倍。

Conclusion: Bio-MF实现快速EEG-to-fNIRS合成，同时保持任务相关生成质量，可支撑下游混合MI解码。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [214] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了用神经网络参数化的随机微分方程(SDE)建模类星体光变曲线的历史,并提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降(CDM-SGD)及连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面巡天数据中类星体光变曲线呈随机变化,建模其随机性(反映超大质量黑洞信息)是统计难题,需借助SDE与神经网络参数化方法。

Method: 1) 系统综述神经网络参数化SDE在类星体光变建模中的适配;2) 提出依赖离散迭代过程历史状态的Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD;3) 提出无需解HJB偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构用于探索性策略。

Result: 在二维景观仿真中,CDM-SGD相比Vanilla SGD通过调整超参数表现出更广的探索与更精确的收敛行为;所提强化学习结构的最优性条件可还原既有工作的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: 延迟记忆与连续时间策略梯度为SDE建模与探索性优化提供了有效途径,并与已有理论保持一致。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [215] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究RydbergGPT在量子比特测量数据上的神经缩放律，发现临界点附近损失-数据量关系符合幂律，且临界统计特性最接近自然语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经缩放律是否依赖模型-数据对的性质，并以量子多体临界数据与自然语言作对比，验证多尺度依赖性对稳定缩放的作用。

Method: 训练自回归Transformer（RydbergGPT）于相互作用Rydberg原子阵列的量子比特投影测量数据；分析损失随训练集大小的幂律拟合（含loss floor修正）；用熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息“两点”函数比较量子测量与自然语言语料的统计结构。

Result: 临界点附近损失-数据量关系良好符合带损失下限修正的幂律，远离临界点时幂律描述质量显著下降；临界两点函数统计最接近自然语言，远离临界点则衰减更快。

Conclusion: 支持多尺度依赖性有助于稳定神经缩放的假设，缩放行为应视为模型-数据对的属性。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [216] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究AI同行评审的递归反馈循环，发现引入合成评审会导致评分分布压缩和语义多样性下降，称为"科学判断崩溃"，并提出TrustReviewer系统从训练和测试两个阶段进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多参与科学评估，其生成的评审进入公开数据和训练语料后，会导致后续评审学习先前模型的判断，形成递归循环，可能损害评审的多样性和质量。

Method: 从Llama 3.1 8B出发，先在2018-2023年ICLR官方评审上微调评审模型，再用2024年ICLR数据以不同比例的官方与模型生成评审混合训练四个后继模型。提出TrustReviewer系统：训练阶段用精选语料单阶段训练以减少低质量监督；测试阶段用配对激活引导进行纠正。

Result: 引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，并降低同论文层面和语料库层面的语义多样性，即"科学判断崩溃"现象。TrustReviewer的干预有助于保持判断多样性并改善推荐对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了递归评审训练的具体风险，并提供实用干预措施以保持判断多样性、改善AI辅助科学评估中的推荐对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [217] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出端到端基于模型的强化学习算法，通过同步LDBA与BAMDP实现未知环境下LTL规范的高效策略合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中按LTL规范（如安全、可达性）高效合成策略，传统非贝叶斯方法探索-利用权衡不佳，需要更好的探索机制来提升样本效率。

Method: 将LTL任务的LDBA与环境表示的BAMDP同步，利用贝叶斯RL改善探索-利用权衡；并提出新的BAMCP算法在这种同步BAMDP结构上实现近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。

Result: 在有限和无限视界任务实验中，相比传统无模型方法，在属性满足与样本效率上更优；消融研究验证新BAMCP优于经典BAMCP；并成功应用于"谨慎"RL以减少训练中的违规次数。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效、高效地在未知环境中合成满足LTL规范的策略，并支持减少任务违规的安全训练应用。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [218] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单框架，将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化，利用现成的切换遗憾算法直接导出动态遗憾界，并匹配三种损失类型下的极小极大最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾的重要性日益凸显，但针对强凸和指数凹损失达到最优界通常需要复杂分析。作者希望提供一种更简单、通用的方法来获得最优动态遗憾界。

Method: 核心思想是归约：对任意比较器序列，构造一个每轮无偏的辅助随机序列，并控制其方差和切换次数；再结合合适的替代损失，将动态遗憾分解为对该随机序列的期望切换遗憾与其受控方差之和，从而可用现成的切换遗憾算法求解。

Result: 对强凸和指数凹损失，建立了 \(\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})\) 的动态遗憾界；对一般凸损失，同样归约恢复了 \(O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})\) 的动态遗憾界。这些结果均与三类损失的极小极大最优结果相匹配。

Conclusion: 该框架通过简单的归约统一处理多种损失类型，避免了复杂分析，同时达到最优动态遗憾界，体现了方法的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [219] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一套将竞争性地质解释转化为空间先验，并用水头观测排序其一致性的工作流，缓解早期地下决策中的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高影响地下决策常面临数据稀缺，同一地质系统的不同专家解释难以在项目早期客观判别，往往需钻多口井（耗资数百万美元）才能澄清。现有评估方法依赖主观判断或早期难以获得的密集数据。

Method: 将竞争性地质解释转为备选空间先验；用文本到图像基础模型为每种解释生成1600张地质图像集成，并训练专属变分自编码器提供潜表示；监督式反演网络将水头观测映射到该潜空间，经冻结解码器生成图像并转为对数渗透率场；进行稳态流动模拟获得预测水头，将失配转换为高斯型兼容性得分。

Result: 在基于Johansen Formation的合成基准上，对三种一致性递减的解释测试925个案例，平均水头RMSE分别为0.197（Precise & Accurate）、0.227（Accurate）和0.280（Mismatched）。应用于WIPP的Culebra Dolomite Member两个已发表概念模型，修订模型兼容性权重0.991，原模型0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流可在数据稀缺的早期阶段客观区分竞争性地质解释，与独立证据吻合，为地下决策提供实用依据。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation.
